#########################################################################
# The US intervention chain
#########################################################################

event = {
	id = 500
	random = no
	country = USA

	name = EVT_500_NAME
	desc = EVT_500_DESC
	style = 2

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			#atwar = JAP
			atwar = SOV
			atwar = GER
			atwar = VIC
			atwar = ENG
			#atwar = ITA
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = U08 value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = RUS value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = SOV value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = ENG value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = VIC value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = U09 value = 50 }
			belligerence = { country = OTT value = 50 }
			#belligerence = { country = ITA value = 50 }
			event = 2003010 #Anschluss
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = july year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_500_ACTA
		command = { type = relation which = GER value = -10 }
		command = { type = relation which = ITA value = -10 }
		command = { type = relation which = JAP value = -10 }
	}
}

#########################################################################
# The USA intervention chain
#########################################################################

event = {
	id = 501
	random = no
	country = USA

	name = EVT_501_NAME
	desc = EVT_501_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = ENG country = GER }
			war = { country = VIC country = GER }
			war = { country = SOV country = GER }
		}
		NOT = {
			war = { country = USA country = GER }
			war = { country = USA country = ENG }
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = ITA value = 50 } 
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		ai = no #currently only for player
		OR = {
			war = { country = ENG country = GER }
			war = { country = VIC country = GER }
			war = { country = SOV country = GER }
		}
		NOT = {
			war = { country = USA country = GER }
			war = { country = USA country = ENG }
		}
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 250 } 
			belligerence = { country = ITA value = 250 } 
			AND = {
				OR = {
					control = { province = 56 data = GER }
					alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
				}
				OR = {
					flag = ENG_invaded
					#USSR is losing the war
					NOT = { control = { province = 175 data = SOV } } #Moscow
					NOT = { control = { province = 187 data = SOV } } #Leningrad
					NOT = { control = { province = 163 data = SOV } } #Stalingrad
				}
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_501_ACTA
		command = { type = dissent value = 8 } #Minimum 8 dissent
		#+ up to 92 dissent
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = GER value = 100 } 
							belligerence = { country = ITA value = 100 } 
							control = { province = 485 data = GER }
							alliance = { country = POL country = GER }
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = GER value = 150 } 
							belligerence = { country = ITA value = 150 } 
							control = { province = 56 data = GER }
							alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = GER value = 200 } 
							belligerence = { country = ITA value = 200 } 
							AND = {
								OR = {
									control = { province = 56 data = GER }
									alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
								}
								OR = {
									#Balkans/Spain in Axis
									alliance = { country = SPA country = GER }
									alliance = { country = YUG country = GER }
									alliance = { country = GRE country = GER }
									alliance = { country = CRO country = GER }
									control = { province = 341 data = GER } #Madrid
									control = { province = 401 data = GER } #Athens
									control = { province = 401 data = ITA } #Athens
									control = { province = 382 data = GER } #Zagreb
									#USSR is invaded
									NOT = { control = { province = 212 data = SOV } } #Minsk
									NOT = { control = { province = 238 data = SOV } } #Kiev
								}
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = GER value = 250 } 
							belligerence = { country = ITA value = 250 } 
							AND = {
								OR = {
									control = { province = 56 data = GER }
									alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
								}
								OR = {
									flag = ENG_invaded
									#USSR is losing the war
									NOT = { control = { province = 175 data = SOV } } #Moscow
									NOT = { control = { province = 187 data = SOV } } #Leningrad
									NOT = { control = { province = 163 data = SOV } } #Stalingrad
								}
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		
		
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } } type = alliance which = ENG value = 1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value =  80 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value =  90 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 100 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 110 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 120 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 170 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 100 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 110 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 120 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 130 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 140 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 150 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 160 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 170 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 180 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 190 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = GER } } } type = war which = GER }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 502
	random = no
	country = USA

	name = EVT_502_NAME
	desc = EVT_502_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
			war = { country = VIC country = JAP }
			war = { country = SOV country = JAP }
		}
		NOT = {
			war = { country = USA country = JAP }
			war = { country = USA country = ENG }
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		belligerence = { country = JAP value = 50 } 
	}
	trigger = {
		ai = no #currently only for player
		OR = {
			war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
			war = { country = VIC country = JAP }
			war = { country = SOV country = JAP }
		}
		NOT = {
			war = { country = USA country = JAP }
			war = { country = USA country = ENG }
		}
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 250 } 
			AND = { 
				exists = U87
				NOT = { exists = CHI }
			}
			AND = {
				war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
				#China is losing the war
				NOT = { region = { region = "China" country = CHI data = 1 value = 20 } }
			}
			AND = {
				war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
				war = { country = ENG country = GER }
			}
			AND = {
				#invading USSR
				OR = {
					control = { province = 1372 data = JAP } #Vladivostok
					control = { province = 1372 data = MAN } #Vladivostok
					control = { province = 1366 data = JAP } #Khabarovsk
					control = { province = 1366 data = MAN } #Khabarovsk
					control = { province = 1520 data = JAP } #Chita
					control = { province = 1520 data = MAN } #Chita
					control = { province = 1404 data = JAP } #Ulanbataar
					control = { province = 1404 data = MAN } #Ulanbataar
					control = { province = 1404 data = MEN } #Ulanbataar
				}
				belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_502_ACTA
		command = { type = dissent value = 8 } #Minimum 8 dissent
		#+ up to 92 dissent
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
							AND = { 
								exists = U87
								NOT = { exists = CHI }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
								NOT = { region = { region = "China" country = CHI data = 1 value = 50 } }
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = JAP value = 150 } 
							AND = { 
								exists = U87
								NOT = { exists = CHI }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
								NOT = { region = { region = "China" country = CHI data = 1 value = 40 } }
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = JAP value = 200 } 
							AND = { 
								exists = U87
								NOT = { exists = CHI }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
								#China is losing the war
								NOT = { region = { region = "China" country = CHI data = 1 value = 30 } }
							}
							control = { province = 1337 data = JAP }
							alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP }
							AND = {
								war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
								war = { country = ENG country = GER }
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = JAP value = 250 } 
							AND = { 
								exists = U87
								NOT = { exists = CHI }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
								#China is losing the war
								NOT = { region = { region = "China" country = CHI data = 1 value = 20 } }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
								war = { country = ENG country = GER }
							}
							AND = {
								#invading USSR
								OR = {
									control = { province = 1372 data = JAP } #Vladivostok
									control = { province = 1372 data = MAN } #Vladivostok
									control = { province = 1366 data = JAP } #Khabarovsk
									control = { province = 1366 data = MAN } #Khabarovsk
									control = { province = 1520 data = JAP } #Chita
									control = { province = 1520 data = MAN } #Chita
									control = { province = 1404 data = JAP } #Ulanbataar
									control = { province = 1404 data = MAN } #Ulanbataar
									control = { province = 1404 data = MEN } #Ulanbataar
								}
								belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		
		
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = JAP country = ENG } }	type = alliance which = ENG value = 1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value =  80 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value =  90 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 100 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 110 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 120 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 170 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 100 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 110 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 120 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 130 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 140 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 150 } } type = dissent value = 2  }

# Squeezing Japan
# 1 (1937)

event = {
	id = 510
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_84_NAME
	desc = EVT_510_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 90 } #3 months
			}
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 40 } 
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 20 }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 90 } #3 months
			}
		}
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 40 } 
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 20 }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_510_ACTA
		command = { 																											type = dissent value = 0.5 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value =  80 } } 	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value =  90 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 100 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 110 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 120 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 170 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 100 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 110 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 120 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 130 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 140 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 150 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 160 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 170 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 180 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 190 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		
		command = { type = supplies value = -1500 }
		command = { type = oilpool value = -250 }		
		command = { type = money value = 50 }
		
		command = { type = trade which = USA where = CHI supplies = -10 money = 0.5 value = 1 } 
		command = { type = setflag which = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI0 }
		command = { type = setflag which = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 }
		
		command = { type = relation which = CHI value =  10 }
		command = { type = relation which = JAP value = -10 }
		command = { type = trigger which = 511 }
		command = { type = trigger which = 512 }
	}
}

# 2 (1938)

event = {
	id = 520
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_520_NAME
	desc = EVT_520_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 510
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 180 } #no less than 6 months
			}
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			event = 2049019 #Panay incident, Japan refused to apologize
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 70 } 
			event = { id = 1330110081 days = 360 } #1 year anniversary
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 27 }
			NOT = { flag = OperationZ } #USSR cancelled aid?
			control = { province = 7027 data = JAP } #North Indochina
			control = { province = 1337 data = JAP } #South Indochina
			alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP } #Puppet Indochina
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		event = 510
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 180 } #no less than 6 months
			}
		}
		OR = {
			event = 2049019 #Panay incident, Japan refused to apologize
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 70 } 
			event = { id = 1330110081 days = 360 } #1 year anniversary
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 27 }
			NOT = { flag = OperationZ } #USSR cancelled aid?
			control = { province = 7027 data = JAP } #North Indochina
			control = { province = 1337 data = JAP } #South Indochina
			alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP } #Puppet Indochina
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_511_ACTA
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } } 												type = dissent value = 0.5 } #arms industry and isolationists are upset
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value =  80 } } 	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value =  90 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

# 3 (1939)

event = {
	id = 530
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_530_NAME
	desc = EVT_530_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 520
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 270 } #no less than 9 months
			}
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			event = 2049019 #Panay incident, Japan refused to apologize
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 90 } 
			event = { id = 1330110081 days = 720 } #2 year anniversary
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 35 }
			NOT = { flag = OperationZ } #USSR cancelled aid?
			control = { province = 7027 data = JAP } #North Indochina
			control = { province = 1337 data = JAP } #South Indochina
			alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP } #Puppet Indochina
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				#invading USSR
				OR = {
					control = { province = 1372 data = JAP } #Vladivostok
					control = { province = 1372 data = MAN } #Vladivostok
					control = { province = 1366 data = JAP } #Khabarovsk
					control = { province = 1366 data = MAN } #Khabarovsk

					control = { province = 1520 data = MAN } #Chita
					control = { province = 1404 data = JAP } #Ulanbataar
					control = { province = 1404 data = MAN } #Ulanbataar
					control = { province = 1404 data = MEN } #Ulanbataar
				}
				belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
			}
		}
	}

		event = 520

			event = { id = 1330110081 days = 720 } #2 year anniversary
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 35 }

			control = { province = 7027 data = JAP } #North Indochina

			alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP } #Puppet Indochina
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value =  80 } } 	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value =  90 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 110 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

# 5 (1941 / North Indochina / USSR Operation Z cancelled)

event = {
	id = 550
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_550_NAME
	desc = EVT_550_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 540
		war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			event = { id = 1330110081 days = 450 } #no less than 15 months
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 130 } 
			#event = { id = 1330110081 days = 1440 } #4 year anniversary (maybe too harsh? If Japan and China are stalemated and Japan isn't making any aggressive moves, there is no need for the US to escalate either)
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 50 }
			NOT = { flag = OperationZ } #USSR cancelled aid? (~february to july 1941)
			control = { province = 7027 data = JAP } #North Indochina (september 1940)
			control = { province = 1337 data = JAP } #South Indochina
			alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP } #Puppet Indochina
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
			}
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		event = 540
		war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			event = { id = 1330110081 days = 450 } #no less than 15 months
		}
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 130 }

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 120 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

event = {
	id = 590
	random = no
	country = SOV
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_590_NAME
	desc = EVT_590_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		NOT = { war = { country = JAP } }
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
			war = { country = JAP country = CHC }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = USA }
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
			AND = {
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = JAP } }
				belligerence = { country = JAP value = 70 } 
			}
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		NOT = { war = { country = JAP } }
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
			war = { country = JAP country = CHC }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = USA }
		}
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
			AND = {
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = JAP } }
				belligerence = { country = JAP value = 70 } 
			}
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } }
			war = { country = GER country = ENG }
			event = 2003202 #Potsdam
		}
		OR = {
			#japan is close to surrender
			nuked = { country = JAP data = -2 }
			lost_national = { country = JAP value = 20 } 
		
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 280 } 
			#Japan getting close to victory in China despite the aid
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 55  }
			#Japan destroying the main CCP base
			area = { area = "Shaanxi" country = JAP data = 1 value = 70 }
			area = { area = "Shaanxi" country = U87 data = 1 value = 70 }
			
			AND = {
				#Japan has almost no defenses on the border. Unfortunately, cannot count allied troops, counting only Japanese
				NOT = { ai = JAP }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = JAP province = 1379 type = land size = 12 area = 3 } } #Manchuria
				NOT = { garrison = { country = JAP province = 1199 type = land size = 15 area = 2 } } #Korea
				war = { country = JAP country = ENG } #Japan distracted
				war = { country = JAP country = USA }
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				#USSR has plenty of troops on the border
				garrison = { country = SOV province = 1525 type = land size = 15 area = 2 }
			}
		}
		
		#control = { province = 123 data = SOV } #Terijoki (Finland defeated OR gave in)
		NOT = { 
			#war = { country = GER }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = ENG }
			#war = { country = FIN }
			#war = { country = SWE }
			#war = { country = ROM }
		}
	
		NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 10 } }
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
			# Finished reogranization of the Red Army (~Nov 1942) and ready to strike
			event = { id = 2007030 days = 30 } 
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_590_ACTA
		command = { 														   type = dissent value = 0.5 } #Foreign adventure, not a clear existential threat
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value =  80 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value =  90 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 100 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 110 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 120 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 170 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		
		command = { type = belligerence value = 1 }
		
		command = { type = clrflag which = JAP_SOV_NAP }
		
		#Will give net +1 dissent if there was a non-aggression pact. Otherwise, no dissent.
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = JAP country = SOV } } type = local_setflag which = JapSovNAP }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = JAP } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = dissent value = -4 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = belligerence which = SOV	value = 5  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = belligerence which = JAP	value = -5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = local_clrflag which = JapSovNAP }

		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = MAN } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = dissent value = -5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = local_clrflag which = JapSovNAP }

		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = MEN } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = dissent value = -5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = local_clrflag which = JapSovNAP }
		
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = U87 country = SOV } } type = local_setflag which = JapSovNAP }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = U87 } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = dissent value = -5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = local_clrflag which = JapSovNAP }
		
		command = { type = end_trades which = SOV where = JAP }
		
		
		#For some mysterious reason this counts as a manual declaration of war, resulting in additional +11.5 dissent.
		command = { trigger = { atwar = yes }			type = local_setflag which = atWarSov }
		command = { 									type = war 		which = JAP }

		command = { type = add_claim_area	which = "Liaoning"			where = SOV }
		command = { type = add_claim_area	which = "Rehe"				where = SOV }
		command = { type = add_claim_area	which = "Chahar"			where = SOV }
		command = { type = add_claim_area	which = "Suiyuan_Ningxia"	where = SOV }
		command = { type = add_claim_area	which = "Rehe"				where = SOV }

# Squeezing Germany
# 1 (1939)

	id = 610

	country = USA

	desc = EVT_610_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = GER country = ENG }

			war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		}

		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = VIC } } type = trade which = USA where = VIC energy = -25 metal = -15 rare_materials = -5 money = 0.6 	supplies = -80	value = 1 } 
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = VIC } } type = free_ic 	value = -10 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = VIC } } type = extra_tc value = -50 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = VIC } } type = setflag which = USA_Lend_Lease_to_FRA2 }
		
		command = { type = relation which = ENG value =  20 }
		command = { type = relation which = VIC value =  20 }
		command = { type = relation which = GER value = -20 }
		command = { type = relation which = ITA value = -20 }
		
		#A player cannot cancel embargoes, but he can manually make and break deals. Actual anti-Axis embargo will be handled by a separate AI event.
		command = { type = end_trades which = USA where = GER }
		command = { type = end_trades which = USA where = ITA }

		
		command = { type = trigger which = 631 }
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = VIC } } type = trigger which = 632 }
		command = { type = trigger which = 633 }
	}
}

# 4 (1941 September)

event = {
	id = 640
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_640_NAME
	desc = EVT_640_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = GER country = ENG }
			war = { country = GER country = VIC }
			war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		}
		NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
		event = 630
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 230 } 
			flag = ENG_invaded
			#USSR is invaded
			AND = {
				war = { country = SOV country = GER }
				NOT = { control = { province = 212 data = SOV } } #Minsk
				NOT = { control = { province = 238 data = SOV } } #Kiev
			}
			AND = {
				event = { id = 630 days = 210 } #7 months after Lend-Lease
				OR = {
					NOT = { flag = NOUSW_GER }
					NOT = { flag = NOUSW_ITA }
					#roughly everything west of the 26th meridian
					garrison = { country = GER province = 6580 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 6576 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 6905 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 7470 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 2315 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 2355 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 7493 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 4386 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 2542 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
				}
			}
			event = 2001251 #Germany rejected British peace offer
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = GER country = ENG }
			war = { country = GER country = VIC }

				war = { country = SOV country = GER }
				NOT = { control = { province = 212 data = SOV } } #Minsk
				NOT = { control = { province = 238 data = SOV } } #Kiev
			}
			AND = {
				event = { id = 630 days = 210 } #7 months after Lend-Lease
				OR = {
					flag = USW_GER
					flag = USW_ITA
					#roughly everything west of the 26th meridian

			event = 2001251 #Germany rejected British peace offer
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_640_ACTA
		command = { 																											type = dissent value = 3.0 } #Germans and isolationists are upset. Pearl harbor is -3 dissent default.

			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = SOV }
			war = { country = JAP }
			AND = {
				war = { country = GER }
				NOT = { flag = GER_USA_undeclared_war }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			alliance = { country = ENG }

			access = { country = ENG country = USA }
			access = { country = VIC country = USA }
			access = { country = SPR country = USA }
			access = { country = IRE country = USA }
			access = { country = POR country = USA }
			access = { country = ITA country = USA }
			access = { country = NOR country = USA }
			access = { country = SWE country = USA }
			access = { country = U02 country = USA }
			access = { country = SOV country = USA }
			garrison = { country = USA province = 19 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #England
			garrison = { country = USA province = 1116	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Scotland
			garrison = { country = USA province = 30	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Ireland
			garrison = { country = USA province = 56 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #France
			garrison = { country = USA province = 341 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Spain
			garrison = { country = USA province = 336 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Portugal
			garrison = { country = USA province = 47 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Benelux
			garrison = { country = USA province = 107 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Norway
			garrison = { country = USA province = 101 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Sweden
			garrison = { country = USA province = 515 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Italy
			garrison = { country = USA province = 949 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Algeria
			garrison = { country = USA province = 958 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Morocco
			garrison = { country = USA province = 900 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Egypt
			garrison = { country = USA province = 920 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Libya
			garrison = { country = USA province = 1469 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #India
			garrison = { country = USA province = 1337 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #Indochina
			garrison = { country = USA province = 1237 	type = land size = 1 area = 2 } #China
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30 #Do not want to check this every day like TRP does, too many garrison checks may put way too much pressure on the engine
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_643_ACTA
		ai_chance = 100
		command = { type = dissent value = 5.0 } #correction gives -2
		command = { type = leave_alliance when = 0 }
		command = { type = end_access which = ENG when = 1 }
		command = { type = end_access which = VIC when = 1 }
		command = { type = end_access which = SPR when = 1 }
		command = { type = end_access which = IRE when = 1 }
		command = { type = end_access which = POR when = 1 }
		command = { type = end_access which = ITA when = 1 }
		command = { type = end_access which = NOR when = 1 }
		command = { type = end_access which = SWE when = 1 }
		command = { type = end_access which = U02 when = 1 }
		command = { type = end_access which = SOV when = 1 }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = CongressAdjourns }
		command = { type = event which = 644 where = USA when = 30 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "I am the Senate!"
		ai_chance = 0
		command = { type = dissent  value = 30.0 }
		command = { trigger = { policy = { type = political_left value = 6 } } 				type = set_domestic which = political_left value = 8 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { policy = { type = political_left value = 6 } } }	type = set_domestic which = political_left value = 3 }
		command = { type = local_setflag which = IamTheSenate }
	}
}

		command = { type = local_clrflag which = CongressAdjourns } #the original event can fire again
	}
}

		}
		OR = {

			lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 12 }
			AND = {
				NOT = { control = { province = 212 data = SOV } } #Minsk
				NOT = { control = { province = 238 data = SOV } } #Kiev
			}
		}

		}
		OR = {

				NOT = { control = { province = 212 data = SOV } } #Minsk

#Soviet Aid to China

#Operation Z (1937)

event = {
	id = 750
	random = no
	country = SOV
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_750_NAME
	desc = EVT_750_DESC
	style = 2
  	picture = "ccip_ccpsov"

	decision = {
		war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
		NOT = { war = { country = CHI country = SOV } }
		NOT = { 
			war = { country = GER }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = MAN country = SOV } }
			}
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = 2012081
		non_aggression = { country = CHI country = SOV }
		NOT = { non_aggression = { country = JAP country = SOV } }
		alliance = { country = CHI country = CHC }
		NOT = { flag = JAP_SOV_NAP }
		OR = {
			event = { id = 1330110081 days = 90 } #3 months
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 50 } 
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 25 }
		}
		supplies = 6000
	}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
		NOT = { war = { country = CHI country = SOV } }
		NOT = { 
			war = { country = GER }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = MAN country = SOV } }
			}
		}

#US aid to China adjustment/termination

event = {
	id = 1000
	random = no
	country = USA
	persistent = yes

	name = EVT_1000_NAME
	desc = EVT_1000_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 550
		OR = {
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI0
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI3
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI4
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = { id = 550 days = 30 }
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI0
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI3
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI4
		}
		event = { id = 550 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 }
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				flag = { which = japChi when = -2	value = 35 }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2 }
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 35 }
				flag = { which = japChi when = -2	value = 45 }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI3 }
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 45 }
				flag = { which = japChi when = -2	value = 50 }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI4 }
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 50 }
			}
			AND = {
				OR = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI0 flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2 flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI3 flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI4 }
				OR = { 
					NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = CHI } }
					lost_national = { value = 10 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 20
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	id = 1015

		NOT = { flag = JAP_SOV_NAP }

		event = 2011023 #Khalkin Ghol
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = SOV } }
		#Human SOV has to have at least 30 divisions in the far east region, otherwise Japan is not interested
		garrison = { country = SOV province = 1372 type = land size = 30 area = 2 }
		OR = {
			NOT = { ai = JAP }
			AND = {
				event = 2011014 #Khalkin Ghol - Soviet Victory
				event = 540 #US export control act - war against the US is looming
			}
		}
		OR = {
			event = 2011016 #Khalkin Ghol - Japanese Victory
			NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = CHI } }
			NOT = { alliance = { country = CHC country = CHI } }
			flag = NewFourthArmyIncident
			lost_national = { country = SOV value = 10 }
			war = { country = SOV country = GER }

				event = { id = 2002008 days = 180 } #~6 months since the fall of France. The USSR is getting worried about the looming German invasion

		OR = {

			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG1

event = {
	id = 1040
	random = no
	country = USA
	persistent = yes

	name = EVT_1040_NAME
	desc = EVT_1040_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 710
		OR = {
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV0
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4
		}
	}

		event = { id = 710 days = 30 }
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV0

			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4
		}
		event = { id = 710 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 }
				NOT = { flag = SOV_noLendLease }
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 5 } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 }
				NOT = { flag = SOV_noLendLease }
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 5 }
				NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 20 } }
			}

				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3 }

#UK aid to USSR adjustment/termination

event = {
	id = 1050
	random = no
	country = ENG
	persistent = yes

	name = EVT_1050_NAME
	desc = EVT_1050_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 730
		OR = {
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV0
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = { id = 730 days = 30 }
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV0
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4
		}
		event = { id = 730 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 }
				NOT = { flag = SOV_noLendLease }
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 5 } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 }
				NOT = { flag = SOV_noLendLease }
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 5 }
				NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 20 } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3 }
				NOT = { flag = SOV_noLendLease }
				flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG2
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }

				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }

			}

				OR = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV0 flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3 flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4 }
				OR = { 
					NOT = { war = { country = GER country = SOV } }
					lost_national = { value = 10 }
					flag = SOV_noLendLease
				}
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 60
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_1000_LOW
		trigger = {
			NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 }
			OR = {
				ai = no
				AND = {
					war = { country = GER country = SOV }
					NOT = { flag = SOV_noLendLease }
					NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
					NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 5 } }
				}
			}
		}
		
		command = { type = end_trades which = ENG where = SOV }
		
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 } type = belligerence value = -1  }
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 } type = relation 	which = SOV value = -10 }
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 } type = relation 	which = GER value =  10 }
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 } type = clrflag 		which = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 } type = belligerence value = -2  }
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 } type = free_ic  	value = 10  }
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 } type = extra_tc 	value = 50  }

	}
}

event = {
	id = 2045010
	random = no
	country = ENG

	decision = {
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = {		
			alliance = { country = USA country = GER }
			alliance = { country = USA country = SOV }
		}
		OR = {
			control = { province = 2632 data = ENG }
			control = { province = 1 	data = ENG }
			control = { province = 7139 data = ENG }
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = 620 # Destroyers for bases
	}
	trigger = {
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = {		
			alliance = { country = USA country = GER }
			alliance = { country = USA country = SOV }
		}
		OR = {
			control = { province = 2632 data = ENG }
			control = { province = 1 	data = ENG }
			control = { province = 7139 data = ENG }
		}
		event = 620 # Destroyers for bases
	}
	name = "EVT_3705_NAME"
	desc = "EVT_3705_DESC"
	style = 2
  	picture = "news_paper"

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action_a = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_OK # Offer to take control of Greenland and Iceland
		command = { type = trigger which = 2045011 } #USA
	}
}

event = { 
	id = 2045011
	random = no
	country = USA
	
	name = "EVT_3900_NAME"
	desc = "EVT_3900_DESC"
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"	
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_2045011_ACTA
		ai_chance = 100
		command = { type = belligerence value = 1 }
		command = { type = relation which = GER value = -15 }
		command = { type = relation which = DEN value = -15 }
		command = { type = relation which = ENG value = 10 }
		
		#population is too low to really have any revolt risk
		command = { type = add_core_region	which = "Greenland" 	where = USA }
		command = { type = add_core_region	which = "Iceland" 		where = USA }

		command = { type = trigger which = 2045012 }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_2045011_ACTB
		ai_chance = 0
		command = { type = belligerence value = -1 }
		command = { type = relation which = GER value = 15 }
		command = { type = relation which = DEN value = 15 }
		command = { type = relation which = ENG value = -10 }
	}
}

	random = no
	country = ENG
	
	name = "EVT_3900_NAME"

	}

	desc = EVT_2049096_DESC

	trigger = {
		war = { country = JAP }
		war = { country = GER }
		lost_national = { country = USA value = 5 }
		OR = { # lost US soil, lower if the coutry gets nuked.
			lost_national = { country = USA value = 55 }
			AND =  {
				NOT = { nuked = 1 }
				nuked = { country = USA  data = -1 }
				lost_national = { country = USA value = 40 }
			}
			AND =  {
				NOT = { nuked = 1 }
				nuked = { country = USA  data = -2 }
				lost_national = { country = USA value = 23 }
			}
			AND =  {
				NOT = { nuked = 1 }
				nuked = { country = USA  data = -3 }
				lost_national = { country = USA value = 15 }
			}
			AND =  {
				NOT = { nuked = 1 }
				nuked = { country = USA  data = -5 }
				lost_national = { country = USA value = 5 }
			}
		}
	}

	}

event = {
	id = 2049110
	random = no
	country = USA

	name = EVT_2049110_NAME
	desc = EVT_2049110_DESC
	style = 2
  	picture = "manhattan_project"
  	decision_picture = "decision_atomic_research"

	decision = {
		OR = { 
			war = { country = USA country = SOV } 
			war = { country = USA country = GER } 
			war = { country = USA country = JAP } 
			war = { country = USA country = ENG } 
		}
		NOT = { technology = { country = USA value = 5540 } }
	}
	
	decision_trigger = { 
		money = 10000 
		technology = 5490
	}	
	
	trigger = {
		technology = 5490
		NOT = { technology = { country = USA value = 5540 } }
		OR = { 
			war = { country = USA country = SOV } 
			war = { country = USA country = GER } 
			war = { country = USA country = JAP } 
			war = { country = USA country = ENG } 
		}
		random = 30
	}
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 60
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_2049110_NAME
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { technology = { country = USA value = 5500 } } } type = gain_tech which = 5500 } #Isotope Separation Facility
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { technology = { country = USA value = 5510 } } } type = gain_tech which = 5510 } #Nuclear Fuel Analysis
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { technology = { country = USA value = 5520 } } } type = gain_tech which = 5520 } #Experimental Reactor
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { technology = { country = USA value = 5530 } } } type = gain_tech which = 5530 } #Nuclear Reactor Operability
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { technology = { country = USA value = 5540 } } } type = gain_tech which = 5540 } #Nuclear Power Production
		command = { type = money value = -10000 }
		command = { trigger = { ai = yes } type = money	value = 10 }
	}
}
